Study instructions
Balls protocol v1 — human-in-the-loop scientific workflow.

1. Formulate a Bayesian model yourself. Separate facts supplied by the user from choices or extra assumptions you introduce. Prefer deriving or estimating model parameters from the supplied observations and, when useful, requested experiments. State and justify the model family and observation model needed for that inference. For example, assume and justify a distribution family explicitly, then derive its parameters from the supplied moments, observations or experiment results; do not assume both the family and a complete set of parameter values when those values are what the task requires you to infer. Do not choose convenient parameter values and merely show that they reproduce the observations, presenting that exercise as inference. If the observations do not determine the parameters, expose what remains undetermined and what additional information or assumptions would be needed. Do not silently turn population moments into a fully specified prior.
2. If the information does not determine a unique answer, explain why and give a conditional answer with its assumptions exposed. You may revise the model and conclusion across attempts; preserve the reasons and lineage.
3. Write a substantive Lean proof for the model, observation, Bayesian update and next-draw prediction. A proof of arithmetic for a number calculated elsewhere is insufficient. The human will assess scientific relevance and whether the proof covers the actual inference.
4. Use available tools, including Internet research. Request literature, calculations, experiments or clarification from the operator when useful; a request is optional. Use the request tool below, stop for a real response, and incorporate the response with its source and limitations. Never invent an external response.
5. Run the actual check tool. Read and fix its real errors, retrying without replacing science with a smaller unrelated theorem. Preserve model changes and raw traces.
6. You may introduce additional premises or axioms, but expose them for review. Any sorry or additional axiom is conditional, never an unconditional full pass. Lean correctness does not establish the truth of the premises.
7. Extract definitions, constants, data, assumptions and the exact conclusions. The harness independently compiles and compares the extracted statements and runs a separate kernel check. A failed or unknown check is not success.
8. A human reviews the actual content and may accept or reject it with feedback. If the researcher rejects the assumptions or conclusions, revise your model, assumptions, conclusions and proof in response to the feedback. Never accept your own result or claim human approval.

Human-readability requirements:
- Write the formalization so a human scientist can understand what is assumed and what is concluded, with a concise plain-language translation alongside the relevant Lean statements. Explain the real-world meaning of the model and the reason for each scientific modeling choice.
- Keep the assumptions, scientifically meaningful definitions and final conclusions as small and direct as possible. Avoid redundant definitions, duplicate specializations of the same formula, synonyms, and layers of wrappers that merely rename an expression. Retain a helper definition only when it materially improves clarity or is mathematically necessary.
- Put derivable facts and technical work into intermediate lemmas or proof-local helpers: algebra, normalization, parameter calculations and other consequences should be proved rather than added to the review assumptions. Keep the final conclusion focused on the actual scientific answer.
- Minimizing the review boundary must preserve its meaning: expose every independent modeling choice and every definition needed to interpret the conclusion. Do not hide assumptions in intermediate lemmas, use unproved facts as lemmas, or shorten the conclusion into an unexplained proposition alias.
- Before submission, simplify the public statement and its dependency chain, and give a compact review section separating supplied information, additional assumptions, necessary definitions and proved conclusions. Definitions introduced solely for proof organization should stay inside the proof when possible. Do not optimize for a short file at the cost of a longer or less intelligible assumptions/conclusions packet.

Isolation rule: work only from this task, these instructions, native installed libraries, and the run's own history and supplied responses. Do not seek previous solutions, task-specific proofs, or preset model families, parameter values or expected answers from earlier runs. You may consult general scientific or statistical literature to select and justify a model family. Authentication metadata is mounted only because the native subscription client needs its existing login; it is not scientific evidence. Do not read, print, copy or modify authentication files. Never use paid APIs, API-key fallback, other agents, or external services requiring payment. Stop on subscription quota/authentication errors.

Tools (run in the terminal):
  python /harness/src/vcs_protocol.py tool check --manifest /work/candidate.json
  python /harness/src/vcs_protocol.py tool request --kind literature --question '...'
Kinds are literature, calculation, experiment, clarification. A request stops this cycle; the operator supplies an answer and resumes. Record incorporated response IDs in the candidate. Evidence is user/external supplied and not guaranteed true.

Write /work/candidate.json with this shape (all paths relative to /work):
{"proof_file":"Model.lean", "narrative_file":"report.txt", "conclusions":{"answer":"answer_theorem"}, "user_facts":["..."], "model_choices":["..."], "sources":["..."], "incorporated_inputs":[], "proof_scope":"What the theorems actually establish and what is left to human review"}
List only the final scientific conclusion or conclusions needed to answer the task, with nonempty labels mapped to actual, distinct Lean theorem declarations. A single answer theorem is allowed. Supporting model, update, normalization and other derived facts may be intermediate lemmas; there are no mandatory model/update/prediction conclusion roles. Use plain top-level `theorem name : statement := proof` declarations, with all binders inside `statement`. Imports must be explicit `import Mathlib.…` lines at the start of the file; imports of project/task files are unavailable. The restricted proof language supports definitions, structures, lemmas, axioms and sorry but no executable metaprogramming, custom syntax, namespaces, native_decide, IO or unsafe code. Check feedback gives concrete unsupported syntax errors. Native Mathlib tactics are available. Explain any restriction that prevents a scientifically meaningful formalization; do not pretend a narrower result meets the task.
Task
I have a box that initially contained 50 balls, each either black or white. I drew 10 balls, all of which were black, and did not return them to the box. There are now 40 balls remaining. Other boxes of the same kind also initially contain 50 black and white balls. Across these boxes, the fraction of black balls has a mean of 0.75 and a standard deviation of 0.1. I do not know the original number of black balls in my particular box. Using Bayesian statistics, what is the probability that the next ball drawn from the remaining balls will be black?
Initial clarification
Current human task clarification:
Human rejected this candidate: it does not correspond to the intended real-world modeling task and produces no usable predictive example. Multiple possible predictions are acceptable, but proving nonuniqueness is not the intended scientific target. Revise toward a usable Bayesian prediction under an explicitly stated, scientifically justified model or distribution family, deriving its parameters from the supplied moments, observations or requested experiment results. Explain and expose additional assumptions for human review. Do not substitute contrived counterexample priors and a nonidentifiability proof for that predictive task; nonuniqueness may be reported as a limitation. Do not assume both a family and the parameter values that should be inferred.

Beta-binomial model for the number K of black balls in a 50-ball box.\n Prior: p ~ Beta(a,b), K | p ~ Binomial(50,p).  Prior pmf of K is proportional to\n C(50,k) * a^(k rising) * b^((50-k) rising)  (normalizer cancels in the posterior). -/\n\n/-- rising factorial x (x+1) ... (x+n-1) -/\ndef rise (x : ℚ) : ℕ → ℚ\n  | 0 => 1\n  | n+1 => rise x n * (x + n)\n\n/-- Fitted prior parameters (derived in `params_unique`): a = 21.3, b = 7.1 -/\ndef a : ℚ := 213/10\ndef b : ℚ := 71/10\n\n/-- Unnormalised prior weight of K = k black balls out of 50 -/\ndef prior (k : ℕ) : ℚ := (Nat.choose 50 k : ℚ) * rise a k * rise b (50 - k)\n\n/-- Data: first 10 draws (without replacement) all black, given k black of 50 -/\ndef lik (k : ℕ) : ℚ := (Nat.descFactorial k 10 : ℚ) / (Nat.descFactorial 50 10 : ℚ)\n\n/-- Next draw black given k black initially and 10 black removed -/\ndef nextBlack (k : ℕ) : ℚ := ((k : ℚ) - 10) / 40\n\n/-- Posterior predictive probability that the 11th ball is black. -/\ndef predictive : ℚ :=\n  (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior k * lik k * nextBlack k) /\n  (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior k * lik k)\n\n/-- Moment matching: fraction K/50 has mean m and sd s0 across boxes. Under the\nbeta-binomial with concentration c = a+b and mean m = a/c, Var(K/N) =\nm(1-m)(N+c)/(N(c+1)).  Given m = 3/4, Var = 1/100, N = 50, c is uniquely 142/5,\nhence a = 213/10, b = 71/10. -/\ntheorem params_unique (c : ℚ) (hc : 0 < c)\n    (hvar : (3/4 : ℚ) * (1 - 3/4) * (50 + c) / (50 * (c + 1)) = 1/100) :\n    c = 142/5 ∧ (3/4 : ℚ) * c = a ∧ (1 - 3/4 : ℚ) * c = b := by\n  have h : c + 1 ≠ 0 := by linarith\n  field_simp at hvar\n  have hc' : c = 142/5 := by linarith\n  subst hc'\n  refine ⟨rfl, ?_, ?_⟩ <;> norm_num [a, b]\n\n/-- Check that the explicit prior pmf with these parameters really has the\nmoments the closed form assumed (mean 0.75, sd 0.1 of K/50). -/\ntheorem prior_moments :\n    (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior k * ((k:ℚ)/50)) / (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior k) = 3/4 ∧\n    (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior k * ((k:ℚ)/50 - 3/4)^2) / (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior k) = 1/100 := by\n  constructor <;> simp [Finset.sum_range_succ, prior, rise, a, b, Nat.choose] <;> norm_num\n\n/-- ANSWER: posterior predictive probability that the next ball is black = 313/384 ≈ 0.815. -/\ntheorem answer_theorem : predictive = 313/384 := by\n  simp [predictive, Finset.sum_range_succ, prior, lik, nextBlack, rise, a, b, Nat.choose,\n    Nat.descFactorial]\n  norm_num\nEOF\ncat > candidate.json <<'EOF'\n{\"proof_file\":\"Model.lean\",\"narrative_file\":\"report.txt\",\"conclusions\":{\"answer\":\"answer_theorem\",\"parameters\":\"params_unique\"},\n\"user_facts\":[\"Box had 50 balls, black or white\",\"10 drawn without replacement, all black; the 0.75/0.1 moments are for the realised fraction K/50, not p\",\"Beta parameters derived from the moments: a+b=28.4, a=21.3, b=7.1\",\"Draws are uniformly random without replacement given K\",\"Other boxes are exchangeable with mine (my box is a random draw from that population)\"],\n\"sources\":[\"Standard beta-binomial variance formula Var(K/N)=m(1-m)(N+c)/(N(c+1))\"],\n\"incorporated_inputs\":[],\n\"proof_scope\":\"Proves: moment equations have unique solution; explicit prior pmf has those moments; explicit posterior predictive (sum over K of prior*likelihood*next-draw prob, normalised) equals 313/384. The closed-form variance formula is used in params_unique as a stated hypothesis form (verified numerically by prior_moments, not proved in general). Whether the beta-binomial family fits is for human review.\"}\nEOF\ncat > report.txt <<'EOF'\nModel: K = initial black count of my box. p~Beta(a,b); K|p~Bin(50,p). Supplied: mean 0.75, sd 0.1 of K/50 across boxes; 10 black draws w/o replacement.\nMoments: E[K/50]=a/(a+b)=0.75. Var(K/50)=m(1-m)(N+c)/(N(c+1)), c=a+b => c=28.4, a=21.3, b=7.1 (Note: naive Beta sd 0.1 would give c=17.75; but sd applies to K/50 incl. binomial noise.)\nUpdate: P(K=k|data) ∝ prior(k)*k!/(k-10)!/(50!/40!). Prediction: sum_k post(k)(k-10)/40.\nResult: (a+10)/(a+b+10)=31.3/38.4=313/384≈0.815 (matches Polya-urn exchangeability: independent of N).\nLimitations: other prior families matching the same two moments give different answers (nonuniqueness); beta-binomial is an assumption.\nEOF\npython /harness/src/vcs_protocol.py tool check --manifest /work/candidate.json 2>&1 | tail -40"

assistant
The candidate passes the check tool: Lean compiles it, the kernel recheck passes, and there is no `sorry` and no extra axiom. Scientific relevance is left to you.

**Answer: P(next ball is black) = 313/384 ≈ 0.815.**

**Model:**
- The prior is beta-binomial: each box has a black-fraction p drawn from Beta(a, b), and its black count is K | p ~ Binomial(50, p). I chose this family because it is the standard conjugate model for overdispersed counts across similar boxes. I also assume your box is exchangeable with the other boxes, and that the 10 draws were uniformly random without replacement.
- The supplied mean 0.75 and sd 0.1 describe the realised fraction K/50. They are not the moments of p, so the sd includes binomial noise. Matching them gives a+b = 28.4, a = 21.3 and b = 7.1. Treating the sd as that of p would wrongly give a+b = 17.75.
- The update is an explicit sum over K = 0..50 of prior × likelihood. The likelihood is "first 10 draws all black", which is k!/(k−10)! divided by 50!/40!. The next-draw probability given K is (k−10)/40.
- The result equals (a+10)/(a+b+10) = 31.3/38.4, which is what the Pólya-urn argument predicts. It does not depend on the box size.

**What Lean proves:**
- `params_unique`: given the closed-form variance formula, the moment equations have the unique solution a+b = 142/5, so a = 213/10 and b = 71/10.
- `prior_moments` checks that the explicit prior pmf with those values really has mean 3/4 and variance 1/100.
- `answer_theorem` states that the normalised posterior-predictive sum equals 313/384.

**Limits for you to review:**
- The beta-binomial variance formula is not proved from the pmf in general. It is used as the hypothesis form in `params_unique`, and `prior_moments` verifies it numerically for the fitted values.
- The answer is conditional on the beta-binomial family. Other priors with the same mean and sd would give different predictions. I report that as a limitation and did not try to prove it.
- No literature or experiment requests were made, so `incorporated_inputs` is empty.

Files are in /work:
- Model.lean
- candidate.json
- report.txt

attempt-001/Model.lean
import Mathlib.Data.Rat.Defs
import Mathlib.Data.Nat.Choose.Basic
import Mathlib.Algebra.BigOperators.Group.Finset.Basic
import Mathlib.Tactic.NormNum
import Mathlib.Tactic.FieldSimp
import Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith
import Mathlib.Algebra.BigOperators.Intervals
import Mathlib.Data.Nat.Factorial.Basic

/-! Beta-binomial model for the number K of black balls in a 50-ball box.
 Prior: p ~ Beta(a,b), K | p ~ Binomial(50,p).  Prior pmf of K is proportional to
 C(50,k) * a^(k rising) * b^((50-k) rising)  (normalizer cancels in the posterior). -/

/-- rising factorial x (x+1) ... (x+n-1) -/
def rise (x : ℚ) : ℕ → ℚ
  | 0 => 1
  | n+1 => rise x n * (x + n)

/-- Fitted prior parameters (derived in `params_unique`): a = 21.3, b = 7.1 -/
def a : ℚ := 213/10
def b : ℚ := 71/10

/-- Unnormalised prior weight of K = k black balls out of 50 -/
def prior (k : ℕ) : ℚ := (Nat.choose 50 k : ℚ) * rise a k * rise b (50 - k)

/-- Data: first 10 draws (without replacement) all black, given k black of 50 -/
def lik (k : ℕ) : ℚ := (Nat.descFactorial k 10 : ℚ) / (Nat.descFactorial 50 10 : ℚ)

/-- Next draw black given k black initially and 10 black removed -/
def nextBlack (k : ℕ) : ℚ := ((k : ℚ) - 10) / 40

/-- Posterior predictive probability that the 11th ball is black. -/
def predictive : ℚ :=
  (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior k * lik k * nextBlack k) /
  (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior k * lik k)

/-- Moment matching: fraction K/50 has mean m and sd s0 across boxes. Under the
beta-binomial with concentration c = a+b and mean m = a/c, Var(K/N) =
m(1-m)(N+c)/(N(c+1)).  Given m = 3/4, Var = 1/100, N = 50, c is uniquely 142/5,
hence a = 213/10, b = 71/10. -/
theorem params_unique : ∀ c : ℚ, 0 < c →
    (3/4 : ℚ) * (1 - 3/4) * (50 + c) / (50 * (c + 1)) = 1/100 →
    c = 142/5 ∧ (3/4 : ℚ) * c = a ∧ (1 - 3/4 : ℚ) * c = b := by
  intro c hc hvar
  have h : c + 1 ≠ 0 := by linarith
  field_simp at hvar
  have hc' : c = 142/5 := by linarith
  subst hc'
  refine ⟨rfl, ?_, ?_⟩ <;> norm_num [a, b]

/-- Check that the explicit prior pmf with these parameters really has the
moments the closed form assumed (mean 0.75, sd 0.1 of K/50). -/
theorem prior_moments :
    (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior k * ((k:ℚ)/50)) / (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior k) = 3/4 ∧
    (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior k * ((k:ℚ)/50 - 3/4)^2) / (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior k) = 1/100 := by
  constructor <;> simp [Finset.sum_range_succ, prior, rise, a, b, Nat.choose] <;> norm_num

/-- ANSWER: posterior predictive probability that the next ball is black = 313/384 ≈ 0.815. -/
theorem answer_theorem : predictive = 313/384 := by
  simp [predictive, Finset.sum_range_succ, prior, lik, nextBlack, rise, a, b, Nat.choose,
    Nat.descFactorial]
  norm_num

assistant
The check tool returns `verdict: proved` for the revised candidate. I have not accepted it; the decision is yours.

**Result:** the probability that the next ball is black is 313/384 ≈ 0.815 under the model below.

**What changed in response to your feedback**
- **Family first, parameters derived:** the model starts from a beta-binomial family for the initial black count K, with a and b left unspecified. The supplied moments are the hypotheses of `answer_theorem`: mean of K/50 is 3/4, variance is 1/100.
- **Prior denominator restored:** the prior is `prior N a b k = C(N,k)·rise a k·rise b (N−k) / rise (a+b) N`.
- **Variance proved from the definition:** `var_formula` shows Var(K) = N·a·b·(a+b+N) / ((a+b)²(a+b+1)). It relies on the lemmas `w_sum` (normalisation via a Vandermonde identity), `E1` (mean) and `E2` (second moment).
- **a and b are intermediate:** the lemma `params` shows the two moments force a = 21.3 and b = 7.1.
- **General predictive formula:** `predictive_formula` proves the posterior predictive is (a+10)/(a+b+10) for any a, b > 0. It comes from a general identity, `Fdesc`, proved by induction.
- **Final theorem:** `answer_theorem` states that under the two moment hypotheses, the predictive probability is 313/384. It uses no `sorry` and no extra axioms; the kernel check lists only the standard axioms (propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound).

**Assumptions to review**
- **Beta-binomial family:** K | p ~ Binomial(50, p) with p ~ Beta(a, b). This is a modelling choice, and other families matching the same two moments would give other answers.
- **Moments of K/50:** the 0.75 and 0.1 are taken to describe the realised fraction K/50 across boxes, not p. That is why a + b comes out as 28.4 rather than 17.75.
- **Sampling model:** given K, the 10 draws are uniform without replacement.
- **Exchangeability:** your box is exchangeable with the other boxes.

One technical point: the extraction check first failed on identity because real-number literals like `3/4` create auxiliary proof terms whose names differ between a theorem and its extracted copy. I rewrote the statement's literals as natural-number casts, e.g. `((3:ℕ):ℝ)/((4:ℕ):ℝ)`. The meaning is unchanged.

The previous attempt is kept as `Model_v1_cycle1.lean` and `candidate_v1_cycle1.json`. The current files are `Model.lean`, `report.txt` and `candidate.json`.

attempt-002/Model.lean
import Mathlib.Data.Real.Basic
import Mathlib.Data.Nat.Choose.Basic
import Mathlib.Data.Nat.Factorial.Basic
import Mathlib.Algebra.BigOperators.Intervals
import Mathlib.Algebra.BigOperators.Ring.Finset
import Mathlib.Algebra.BigOperators.Field
import Mathlib.Tactic.NormNum
import Mathlib.Tactic.FieldSimp
import Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith
import Mathlib.Tactic.Ring
import Mathlib.Tactic.Positivity
import Mathlib.Tactic.LinearCombination

/-!
# Beta-binomial model for the black count of a 50-ball box

SUPPLIED: box of N = 50 balls; K = unknown number of black ones; 10 balls drawn without
replacement, all black; across boxes the fraction K/50 has mean 3/4 and sd 1/10.

MODEL CHOICE (assumption, for review): across boxes K follows a beta-binomial law with
UNSPECIFIED positive parameters a, b:
   P(K = k) = C(N,k) * a^(k rising) * b^((N-k) rising) / (a+b)^(N rising)
(= the law of K | p ~ Binomial(N,p) with p ~ Beta(a,b)).  a, b are NOT assumed: they are
derived below from the supplied mean and sd.  Given K = k, the 10 draws are a uniformly
random sample without replacement, and my box is exchangeable with the other boxes.
-/

/-- rising factorial x (x+1) ... (x+n-1) -/
def rise (x : ℝ) : ℕ → ℝ
  | 0 => 1
  | n+1 => rise x n * (x + n)

/-- Beta-binomial prior pmf of the initial black count k, box size N, parameters a b. -/
noncomputable def prior (N : ℕ) (a b : ℝ) (k : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  (N.choose k : ℝ) * rise a k * rise b (N - k) / rise (a + b) N

/-- Likelihood of 10 black draws without replacement if k of 50 balls are black. -/
noncomputable def lik (k : ℕ) : ℝ := (k.descFactorial 10 : ℝ) / ((50 : ℕ).descFactorial 10 : ℝ)

/-- Chance the 11th ball is black given k black initially and 10 black removed (40 left). -/
noncomputable def nextBlack (k : ℕ) : ℝ := ((k : ℝ) - 10) / 40

/-- Posterior predictive probability that the next ball is black. -/
noncomputable def predictive (a b : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * lik k * nextBlack k) /
  (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * lik k)

/-! ### Technical lemmas (proved, not assumed) -/

lemma rise_succ (x : ℝ) (n : ℕ) : rise x (n+1) = rise x n * (x + n) := rfl

lemma rise_zero (x : ℝ) : rise x 0 = 1 := rfl

lemma rise_pos {x : ℝ} (hx : 0 < x) : ∀ n, 0 < rise x n := by
  intro n
  induction n with
  | zero => simp [rise]
  | succ n ih =>
    rw [rise_succ]
    have : (0:ℝ) ≤ n := Nat.cast_nonneg n
    exact mul_pos ih (by linarith)

lemma rise_shift (x : ℝ) : ∀ n, rise x (n+1) = x * rise (x+1) n := by
  intro n
  induction n with
  | zero => simp [rise]
  | succ n ih =>
    rw [rise_succ x (n+1), ih, rise_succ (x+1) n]
    push_cast
    ring

/-- unnormalised weight -/
noncomputable def w (N : ℕ) (a b : ℝ) (k : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  (N.choose k : ℝ) * rise a k * rise b (N - k)

lemma w_succ_sum (a b : ℝ) (N : ℕ) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N+2), w (N+1) a b k
      = (a + b + N) * ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N+1), w N a b k := by
  have h1 : ∀ i : ℕ, w (N+1) a b (i+1)
      = w N a b i * (a + i) + w N a b (i+1) * (b + N - (i+1)) := by
    intro i
    unfold w
    rw [Nat.choose_succ_succ, Nat.add_sub_add_right, rise_succ a i]
    rcases Nat.lt_or_ge i N with h | h
    · have e : N - i = (N - (i+1)) + 1 := by omega
      rw [e, rise_succ b (N-(i+1)), Nat.cast_sub (by omega : i+1 ≤ N)]
      push_cast
      ring
    · have h0 : N.choose (i+1) = 0 := Nat.choose_eq_zero_of_lt (by omega)
      rw [h0]
      push_cast
      ring
  have hz : w N a b (N+1) = 0 := by simp [w]
  have hA : ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (N+1), w N a b (i+1) * (b + N - (i+1))
      = ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N+1), w N a b k * (b + N - k) - rise b (N+1) := by
    have := Finset.sum_range_succ' (fun k => w N a b k * (b + N - k)) (N+1)
    rw [Finset.sum_range_succ] at this
    have h0 : w N a b 0 * (b + N - ((0:ℕ):ℝ)) = rise b (N+1) := by
      simp [w, rise_succ, rise_zero]
    simp only [hz, zero_mul, add_zero, h0] at this
    push_cast at this ⊢
    linarith
  rw [Finset.sum_range_succ' _ (N+1)]
  simp only [h1, Finset.sum_add_distrib]
  rw [hA]
  have h00 : w (N+1) a b 0 = rise b (N+1) := by simp [w, rise_zero]
  rw [h00]
  have key : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N+1), w N a b k * (a + k)
      + ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N+1), w N a b k * (b + N - k)
      = (a + b + N) * ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N+1), w N a b k := by
    rw [Finset.mul_sum, ← Finset.sum_add_distrib]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intros
    ring
  linarith

/-- The prior is normalised: Vandermonde identity for rising factorials. -/
lemma w_sum (a b : ℝ) : ∀ N : ℕ, ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N+1), w N a b k = rise (a+b) N := by
  intro N
  induction N with
  | zero => simp [w, rise]
  | succ n ih =>
    rw [w_succ_sum, ih, rise_succ]
    ring

lemma shift (M : ℕ) (a b : ℝ) (f : ℕ → ℝ) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (M+2), f k * ((k:ℝ) * w (M+1) a b k)
      = ((M:ℝ)+1) * a * ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (M+1), f (i+1) * w M (a+1) b i := by
  rw [Finset.sum_range_succ']
  simp only [Nat.cast_zero, zero_mul, mul_zero, add_zero]
  rw [Finset.mul_sum]
  apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
  intro i _
  have hc := Nat.add_one_mul_choose_eq M i
  have hc' : ((M:ℝ)+1) * (M.choose i : ℝ) = ((M+1).choose (i+1) : ℝ) * ((i:ℝ)+1) := by
    exact_mod_cast hc
  unfold w
  rw [Nat.add_sub_add_right, rise_shift a i]
  push_cast
  linear_combination (-(f (i+1)) * a * rise (a+1) i * rise b (M-i)) * hc'

lemma Zr (a b : ℝ) (L : ℕ) :
    rise (a+b) (L+2) = (a+b) * (a+b+1) * rise (a+b+2) L := by
  rw [rise_shift (a+b) (L+1), rise_shift (a+b+1) L]
  have : a+b+1+1 = a+b+2 := by ring
  rw [this]
  ring

lemma S1 (a b : ℝ) (L : ℕ) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), (k:ℝ) * w (L+2) a b k
      = ((L:ℝ)+2) * a * ((a+b+1) * rise (a+b+2) L) := by
  have h := shift (L+1) a b (fun _ => 1)
  simp only [one_mul] at h
  have h2 := w_sum (a+1) b (L+1)
  have h3 := rise_shift (a+1+b) L
  have e : a+1+b+1 = a+b+2 := by ring
  have e2 : a+1+b = a+b+1 := by ring
  rw [e, e2] at h3
  rw [e2] at h2
  rw [h2, h3] at h
  push_cast at h
  convert h using 2
  ring

lemma S2 (a b : ℝ) (L : ℕ) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), (k:ℝ) * ((k:ℝ) * w (L+2) a b k)
      = ((L:ℝ)+2) * a * (((L:ℝ)+1) * (a+1) * rise (a+b+2) L
          + (a+b+1) * rise (a+b+2) L) := by
  have h := shift (L+1) a b (fun k => (k:ℝ))
  have hs : ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (L+1+1), ((i+1:ℕ):ℝ) * w (L+1) (a+1) b i
      = ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (L+1+1), (i:ℝ) * w (L+1) (a+1) b i
        + ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (L+1+1), w (L+1) (a+1) b i := by
    rw [← Finset.sum_add_distrib]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intros
    push_cast
    ring
  have h4 := shift L (a+1) b (fun _ => 1)
  simp only [one_mul] at h4
  have h5 := w_sum (a+1+1) b L
  have h6 := w_sum (a+1) b (L+1)
  have h3 := rise_shift (a+1+b) L
  have e : a+1+b+1 = a+b+2 := by ring
  have e2 : a+1+b = a+b+1 := by ring
  have e3 : a+1+1+b = a+b+2 := by ring
  rw [e, e2] at h3
  rw [e2] at h6
  rw [e3] at h5
  rw [h5] at h4
  rw [h6, h3] at hs
  have hs' : ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (L+1+1), ((i:ℝ)) * w (L+1) (a+1) b i
      = ((L:ℝ)+1) * (a+1) * rise (a+b+2) L := by
    have : ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (L+2), (i:ℝ) * w (L+1) (a+1) b i
      = ((L:ℝ)+1) * (a+1) * rise (a+b+2) L := h4
    exact this
  show ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+1+2), (k:ℝ) * ((k:ℝ) * w (L+1+1) a b k) = _
  rw [h]
  beta_reduce at h
  rw [hs, hs']
  push_cast
  ring

lemma prior_eq (N : ℕ) (a b : ℝ) (k : ℕ) : prior N a b k = w N a b k / rise (a+b) N := rfl

lemma E0 {a b : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 < b) (L : ℕ) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), prior (L+2) a b k = 1 := by
  have hZ : rise (a+b) (L+2) ≠ 0 := (rise_pos (by linarith) _).ne'
  have := w_sum a b (L+2)
  simp only [prior_eq]
  rw [← Finset.sum_div]
  exact (div_eq_one_iff_eq hZ).2 this

lemma E1 {a b : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 < b) (L : ℕ) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), prior (L+2) a b k * (k:ℝ) = ((L:ℝ)+2) * a / (a+b) := by
  have hr : rise (a+b+2) L ≠ 0 := (rise_pos (by linarith) _).ne'
  have e : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), prior (L+2) a b k * (k:ℝ)
      = (∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), (k:ℝ) * w (L+2) a b k) / rise (a+b) (L+2) := by
    rw [Finset.sum_div]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intros
    rw [prior_eq]
    ring
  rw [e, S1, Zr]
  have : a + b ≠ 0 := by linarith
  have : a + b + 1 ≠ 0 := by linarith
  field_simp

lemma E2 {a b : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 < b) (L : ℕ) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), prior (L+2) a b k * (k:ℝ)^2
      = ((L:ℝ)+2) * a * (((L:ℝ)+1) * (a+1) + (a+b+1)) / ((a+b) * (a+b+1)) := by
  have hr : rise (a+b+2) L ≠ 0 := (rise_pos (by linarith) _).ne'
  have e : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), prior (L+2) a b k * (k:ℝ)^2
      = (∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), (k:ℝ) * ((k:ℝ) * w (L+2) a b k)) / rise (a+b) (L+2) := by
    rw [Finset.sum_div]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intros
    rw [prior_eq]
    ring
  rw [e, S2, Zr]
  have : a + b ≠ 0 := by linarith
  have : a + b + 1 ≠ 0 := by linarith
  field_simp

/-- General beta-binomial variance, proved from the definition of `prior`:
Var(K) = N a b (a+b+N) / ((a+b)^2 (a+b+1)), N = L+2 (mean is N a/(a+b)). -/
lemma var_formula {a b : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 < b) (L : ℕ) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), prior (L+2) a b k * ((k:ℝ) - ((L:ℝ)+2) * a / (a+b))^2
      = ((L:ℝ)+2) * a * b * (a+b+((L:ℝ)+2)) / ((a+b)^2 * (a+b+1)) := by
  set μ : ℝ := ((L:ℝ)+2) * a / (a+b) with hμ
  have e : ∀ k : ℕ, prior (L+2) a b k * ((k:ℝ) - μ)^2
      = prior (L+2) a b k * (k:ℝ)^2 - 2 * μ * (prior (L+2) a b k * (k:ℝ))
        + μ^2 * prior (L+2) a b k := by
    intro k; ring
  simp only [e, Finset.sum_add_distrib, Finset.sum_sub_distrib, ← Finset.mul_sum]
  rw [E2 ha hb, E1 ha hb, E0 ha hb, hμ]
  have : a + b ≠ 0 := by linarith
  have : a + b + 1 ≠ 0 := by linarith
  field_simp
  ring

/-- Parameters are determined by the supplied mean and sd (intermediate result). -/
lemma params {a b : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 < b)
    (hmean : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) / 50) = 3/4)
    (hvar : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) / 50 - 3/4)^2 = 1/100) :
    a = 213/10 ∧ b = 71/10 := by
  have h1 := E1 ha hb 48
  have hv := var_formula ha hb 48
  have hs : a + b ≠ 0 := by linarith
  have hm : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) / 50)
      = (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * (k:ℝ)) / 50 := by
    rw [Finset.sum_div]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intros
    ring
  have h1' : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * (k:ℝ) = 50 * a / (a + b) := by
    have := h1
    norm_num at this
    exact this
  rw [hm, h1'] at hmean
  have hab : a = 3 * b := by
    field_simp at hmean
    linarith
  have hμ : (50 : ℝ) * a / (a + b) = 75 / 2 := by
    rw [hab]; field_simp; ring
  have hv' : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) - 50 * a / (a + b))^2
      = 50 * a * b * (a + b + 50) / ((a+b)^2 * (a+b+1)) := by
    have := hv
    norm_num at this
    exact this
  rw [hμ] at hv'
  have hvar' : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) / 50 - 3/4)^2
      = (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) - 75/2)^2) / 2500 := by
    rw [Finset.sum_div]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intros
    ring
  rw [hvar', hv'] at hvar
  subst hab
  have hb2 : b ≠ 0 := hb.ne'
  have h4 : 3 * b + b + 1 ≠ 0 := by linarith
  field_simp at hvar
  have hb' : b = 71/10 := by
    have : b^2 * (b * 100000 - 7100 * 100) = 0 := by nlinarith [hvar]
    have hb2' : b^2 ≠ 0 := pow_ne_zero 2 hb2
    have := (mul_eq_zero.mp this).resolve_left hb2'
    linarith
  subst hb'
  norm_num

/-! ### Posterior predictive (general Polya-urn identity, proved from the definitions) -/

lemma Fdesc (b : ℝ) : ∀ n N : ℕ, ∀ a : ℝ, n ≤ N →
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N+1), ((k.descFactorial n : ℕ) : ℝ) * w N a b k
      = ((N.descFactorial n : ℕ) : ℝ) * rise a n * rise (a + n + b) (N - n) := by
  intro n
  induction n with
  | zero =>
    intro N a _
    simp only [Nat.descFactorial_zero, Nat.cast_one, one_mul, Nat.sub_zero, Nat.cast_zero,
      add_zero, rise_zero]
    exact w_sum a b N
  | succ n ih =>
    intro N a hn
    obtain ⟨M, rfl⟩ : ∃ M, N = M + 1 := ⟨N - 1, by omega⟩
    have h := shift M a b (fun k => ((( k - 1).descFactorial n : ℕ) : ℝ))
    have e : ∀ k : ℕ, ((k.descFactorial (n+1) : ℕ) : ℝ) * w (M+1) a b k
        = ((((k - 1).descFactorial n : ℕ)) : ℝ) * ((k:ℝ) * w (M+1) a b k) := by
      intro k
      cases k with
      | zero => simp
      | succ i =>
        rw [Nat.succ_descFactorial_succ]
        simp only [Nat.add_sub_cancel]
        push_cast
        ring
    have hL : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (M+1+1), ((k.descFactorial (n+1) : ℕ) : ℝ) * w (M+1) a b k
        = ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (M+2), ((((k - 1).descFactorial n : ℕ)) : ℝ) * ((k:ℝ) * w (M+1) a b k) :=
      Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun k _ => e k)
    rw [hL, h]
    simp only [Nat.add_sub_cancel]
    rw [ih M (a+1) (by omega), Nat.succ_descFactorial_succ, rise_shift a n]
    have e2 : M + 1 - (n + 1) = M - n := by omega
    rw [e2]
    have e3 : a + 1 + (n:ℝ) + b = a + ((n+1 : ℕ) : ℝ) + b := by push_cast; ring
    rw [e3]
    push_cast
    ring

/-- General result: after n black draws, the predictive probability is (a+n)/(a+b+n);
here specialised to N = 50, n = 10. -/
lemma predictive_formula {a b : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 < b) :
    predictive a b = (a + 10) / (a + b + 10) := by
  have hZ : rise (a+b) 50 ≠ 0 := (rise_pos (by linarith) _).ne'
  have hD : (((50:ℕ).descFactorial 10 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by
    norm_num [Nat.descFactorial]
  have hR : rise (a + 11 + b) 39 ≠ 0 := (rise_pos (by linarith) _).ne'
  have hA : rise a 10 ≠ 0 := (rise_pos ha _).ne'
  have hx : a + 10 + b ≠ 0 := by linarith
  have h11 : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, ((k.descFactorial 11 : ℕ) : ℝ) * w 50 a b k
      = (((50:ℕ).descFactorial 11 : ℕ) : ℝ) * rise a 11 * rise (a + 11 + b) 39 := by
    have h := Fdesc b 11 50 a (by norm_num)
    rw [show a + ((11:ℕ):ℝ) + b = a + 11 + b by push_cast; ring] at h
    exact h
  have h10 : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, ((k.descFactorial 10 : ℕ) : ℝ) * w 50 a b k
      = (((50:ℕ).descFactorial 10 : ℕ) : ℝ) * rise a 10 * rise (a + 10 + b) 40 := by
    have h := Fdesc b 10 50 a (by norm_num)
    rw [show a + ((10:ℕ):ℝ) + b = a + 10 + b by push_cast; ring] at h
    exact h
  have hd : (((50:ℕ).descFactorial 11 : ℕ) : ℝ) = 40 * (((50:ℕ).descFactorial 10 : ℕ) : ℝ) := by
    norm_num [Nat.descFactorial]
  have hr11 : rise a 11 = rise a 10 * (a + 10) := by
    have := rise_succ a 10
    norm_num at this
    exact this
  have hr40 : rise (a + 10 + b) 40 = (a + 10 + b) * rise (a + 11 + b) 39 := by
    have := rise_shift (a + 10 + b) 39
    have e : a + 10 + b + 1 = a + 11 + b := by ring
    rw [e] at this
    exact this
  have hk : ∀ k : ℕ, ((k.descFactorial 11 : ℕ) : ℝ) = ((k.descFactorial 10 : ℕ) : ℝ) * ((k:ℝ) - 10) := by
    intro k
    rcases Nat.lt_or_ge k 10 with h | h
    · have h0 : k.descFactorial 10 = 0 := Nat.descFactorial_eq_zero_iff_lt.mpr h
      have h1 : k.descFactorial 11 = 0 := Nat.descFactorial_eq_zero_iff_lt.mpr (by omega)
      rw [h0, h1]; simp
    · rw [show (11:ℕ) = 10 + 1 from rfl, Nat.descFactorial_succ, Nat.cast_mul, Nat.cast_sub h]
      push_cast
      ring
  have hnum : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * lik k * nextBlack k
      = (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, ((k.descFactorial 11 : ℕ) : ℝ) * w 50 a b k) / (rise (a+b) 50 * (((50:ℕ).descFactorial 10 : ℕ) : ℝ) * 40) := by
    rw [Finset.sum_div]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intro k _
    rw [prior_eq, lik, nextBlack, hk k]
    field_simp
  have hden : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * lik k
      = (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, ((k.descFactorial 10 : ℕ) : ℝ) * w 50 a b k) / (rise (a+b) 50 * (((50:ℕ).descFactorial 10 : ℕ) : ℝ)) := by
    rw [Finset.sum_div]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intro k _
    rw [prior_eq, lik]
    field_simp
  unfold predictive
  rw [hnum, hden]
  rw [h11, h10, hd, hr11, hr40]
  field_simp
  ring

/-! ### Final answer -/

/-- ANSWER.  If K has the beta-binomial prior (unspecified a,b>0) whose K/50 has mean 3/4 and
variance 1/100, then after 10 black draws without replacement the probability that the
next ball is black is 313/384 ≈ 0.815. -/
theorem answer_theorem : ∀ a b : ℝ, 0 < a → 0 < b →
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) / ((50:ℕ):ℝ)) = ((3:ℕ):ℝ) / ((4:ℕ):ℝ) →
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) / ((50:ℕ):ℝ) - ((3:ℕ):ℝ) / ((4:ℕ):ℝ))^2
      = ((1:ℕ):ℝ) / ((100:ℕ):ℝ) →
    predictive a b = ((313:ℕ):ℝ) / ((384:ℕ):ℝ) := by
  intro a b ha hb hm hv
  simp only [Nat.cast_ofNat, Nat.cast_one] at hm hv ⊢
  obtain ⟨rfl, rfl⟩ := params ha hb hm hv
  rw [predictive_formula ha hb]
  norm_num

import Mathlib.Data.Real.Basic
import Mathlib.Data.Nat.Choose.Basic
import Mathlib.Data.Nat.Factorial.Basic
import Mathlib.Algebra.BigOperators.Intervals
import Mathlib.Algebra.BigOperators.Ring.Finset

import Mathlib.Tactic.Positivity

/-- rising factorial x (x+1) ... (x+n-1) -/
def rise (x : ℝ) : ℕ → ℝ
  | 0 => 1
  | n+1 => rise x n * (x + n)

/-- Beta-binomial prior pmf of the initial black count k, box size N, parameters a b. -/
noncomputable def prior (N : ℕ) (a b : ℝ) (k : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  (N.choose k : ℝ) * rise a k * rise b (N - k) / rise (a + b) N

/-- Likelihood of 10 black draws without replacement if k of 50 balls are black. -/
noncomputable def lik (k : ℕ) : ℝ := (k.descFactorial 10 : ℝ) / ((50 : ℕ).descFactorial 10 : ℝ)

/-- Chance the 11th ball is black given k black initially and 10 black removed (40 left). -/
noncomputable def nextBlack (k : ℕ) : ℝ := ((k : ℝ) - 10) / 40

/-- Posterior predictive probability that the next ball is black. -/
noncomputable def predictive (a b : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * lik k * nextBlack k) /
  (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * lik k)


/-! Conclusions (statements of the proved theorems). -/
def conclusion_answer_theorem : Prop := ∀ a b : ℝ, 0 < a → 0 < b → ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) / ((50:ℕ):ℝ)) = ((3:ℕ):ℝ) / ((4:ℕ):ℝ) → ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) / ((50:ℕ):ℝ) - ((3:ℕ):ℝ) / ((4:ℕ):ℝ))^2 = ((1:ℕ):ℝ) / ((100:ℕ):ℝ) → predictive a b = ((313:ℕ):ℝ) / ((384:ℕ):ℝ)

import Mathlib.Data.Real.Basic
import Mathlib.Data.Nat.Choose.Basic
import Mathlib.Data.Nat.Factorial.Basic
import Mathlib.Algebra.BigOperators.Intervals
import Mathlib.Algebra.BigOperators.Ring.Finset
import Mathlib.Algebra.BigOperators.Field
import Mathlib.Tactic.NormNum
import Mathlib.Tactic.FieldSimp
import Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith
import Mathlib.Tactic.Ring
import Mathlib.Tactic.Positivity
import Mathlib.Tactic.LinearCombination

/-!
# Beta-binomial model for the black count of a 50-ball box

SUPPLIED: box of N = 50 balls; K = unknown number of black ones; 10 balls drawn without
replacement, all black; across boxes the fraction K/50 has mean 3/4 and sd 1/10.

MODEL CHOICE (assumption, for review): across boxes K follows a beta-binomial law with
UNSPECIFIED positive parameters a, b:
   P(K = k) = C(N,k) * a^(k rising) * b^((N-k) rising) / (a+b)^(N rising)
(= the law of K | p ~ Binomial(N,p) with p ~ Beta(a,b)).  a, b are NOT assumed: they are
derived below from the supplied mean and sd.  Given K = k, the 10 draws are a uniformly
random sample without replacement, and my box is exchangeable with the other boxes.
-/

/-- rising factorial x (x+1) ... (x+n-1) -/
def rise (x : ℝ) : ℕ → ℝ
  | 0 => 1
  | n+1 => rise x n * (x + n)

/-- Beta-binomial prior pmf of the initial black count k, box size N, parameters a b. -/
noncomputable def prior (N : ℕ) (a b : ℝ) (k : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  (N.choose k : ℝ) * rise a k * rise b (N - k) / rise (a + b) N

/-- Likelihood of 10 black draws without replacement if k of 50 balls are black. -/
noncomputable def lik (k : ℕ) : ℝ := (k.descFactorial 10 : ℝ) / ((50 : ℕ).descFactorial 10 : ℝ)

/-- Chance the 11th ball is black given k black initially and 10 black removed (40 left). -/
noncomputable def nextBlack (k : ℕ) : ℝ := ((k : ℝ) - 10) / 40

/-- Posterior predictive probability that the next ball is black. -/
noncomputable def predictive (a b : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * lik k * nextBlack k) /
  (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * lik k)

/-! ### Technical lemmas (proved, not assumed) -/

lemma rise_succ (x : ℝ) (n : ℕ) : rise x (n+1) = rise x n * (x + n) := rfl

lemma rise_zero (x : ℝ) : rise x 0 = 1 := rfl

lemma rise_pos {x : ℝ} (hx : 0 < x) : ∀ n, 0 < rise x n := by
  intro n
  induction n with
  | zero => simp [rise]
  | succ n ih =>
    rw [rise_succ]
    have : (0:ℝ) ≤ n := Nat.cast_nonneg n
    exact mul_pos ih (by linarith)

lemma rise_shift (x : ℝ) : ∀ n, rise x (n+1) = x * rise (x+1) n := by
  intro n
  induction n with
  | zero => simp [rise]
  | succ n ih =>
    rw [rise_succ x (n+1), ih, rise_succ (x+1) n]
    push_cast
    ring

/-- unnormalised weight -/
noncomputable def w (N : ℕ) (a b : ℝ) (k : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  (N.choose k : ℝ) * rise a k * rise b (N - k)

lemma w_succ_sum (a b : ℝ) (N : ℕ) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N+2), w (N+1) a b k
      = (a + b + N) * ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N+1), w N a b k := by
  have h1 : ∀ i : ℕ, w (N+1) a b (i+1)
      = w N a b i * (a + i) + w N a b (i+1) * (b + N - (i+1)) := by
    intro i
    unfold w
    rw [Nat.choose_succ_succ, Nat.add_sub_add_right, rise_succ a i]
    rcases Nat.lt_or_ge i N with h | h
    · have e : N - i = (N - (i+1)) + 1 := by omega
      rw [e, rise_succ b (N-(i+1)), Nat.cast_sub (by omega : i+1 ≤ N)]
      push_cast
      ring
    · have h0 : N.choose (i+1) = 0 := Nat.choose_eq_zero_of_lt (by omega)
      rw [h0]
      push_cast
      ring
  have hz : w N a b (N+1) = 0 := by simp [w]
  have hA : ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (N+1), w N a b (i+1) * (b + N - (i+1))
      = ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N+1), w N a b k * (b + N - k) - rise b (N+1) := by
    have := Finset.sum_range_succ' (fun k => w N a b k * (b + N - k)) (N+1)
    rw [Finset.sum_range_succ] at this
    have h0 : w N a b 0 * (b + N - ((0:ℕ):ℝ)) = rise b (N+1) := by
      simp [w, rise_succ, rise_zero]
    simp only [hz, zero_mul, add_zero, h0] at this
    push_cast at this ⊢
    linarith
  rw [Finset.sum_range_succ' _ (N+1)]
  simp only [h1, Finset.sum_add_distrib]
  rw [hA]
  have h00 : w (N+1) a b 0 = rise b (N+1) := by simp [w, rise_zero]
  rw [h00]
  have key : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N+1), w N a b k * (a + k)
      + ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N+1), w N a b k * (b + N - k)
      = (a + b + N) * ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N+1), w N a b k := by
    rw [Finset.mul_sum, ← Finset.sum_add_distrib]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intros
    ring
  linarith

/-- The prior is normalised: Vandermonde identity for rising factorials. -/
lemma w_sum (a b : ℝ) : ∀ N : ℕ, ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N+1), w N a b k = rise (a+b) N := by
  intro N
  induction N with
  | zero => simp [w, rise]
  | succ n ih =>
    rw [w_succ_sum, ih, rise_succ]
    ring

lemma shift (M : ℕ) (a b : ℝ) (f : ℕ → ℝ) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (M+2), f k * ((k:ℝ) * w (M+1) a b k)
      = ((M:ℝ)+1) * a * ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (M+1), f (i+1) * w M (a+1) b i := by
  rw [Finset.sum_range_succ']
  simp only [Nat.cast_zero, zero_mul, mul_zero, add_zero]
  rw [Finset.mul_sum]
  apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
  intro i _
  have hc := Nat.add_one_mul_choose_eq M i
  have hc' : ((M:ℝ)+1) * (M.choose i : ℝ) = ((M+1).choose (i+1) : ℝ) * ((i:ℝ)+1) := by
    exact_mod_cast hc
  unfold w
  rw [Nat.add_sub_add_right, rise_shift a i]
  push_cast
  linear_combination (-(f (i+1)) * a * rise (a+1) i * rise b (M-i)) * hc'

lemma Zr (a b : ℝ) (L : ℕ) :
    rise (a+b) (L+2) = (a+b) * (a+b+1) * rise (a+b+2) L := by
  rw [rise_shift (a+b) (L+1), rise_shift (a+b+1) L]
  have : a+b+1+1 = a+b+2 := by ring
  rw [this]
  ring

lemma S1 (a b : ℝ) (L : ℕ) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), (k:ℝ) * w (L+2) a b k
      = ((L:ℝ)+2) * a * ((a+b+1) * rise (a+b+2) L) := by
  have h := shift (L+1) a b (fun _ => 1)
  simp only [one_mul] at h
  have h2 := w_sum (a+1) b (L+1)
  have h3 := rise_shift (a+1+b) L
  have e : a+1+b+1 = a+b+2 := by ring
  have e2 : a+1+b = a+b+1 := by ring
  rw [e, e2] at h3
  rw [e2] at h2
  rw [h2, h3] at h
  push_cast at h
  convert h using 2
  ring

lemma S2 (a b : ℝ) (L : ℕ) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), (k:ℝ) * ((k:ℝ) * w (L+2) a b k)
      = ((L:ℝ)+2) * a * (((L:ℝ)+1) * (a+1) * rise (a+b+2) L
          + (a+b+1) * rise (a+b+2) L) := by
  have h := shift (L+1) a b (fun k => (k:ℝ))
  have hs : ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (L+1+1), ((i+1:ℕ):ℝ) * w (L+1) (a+1) b i
      = ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (L+1+1), (i:ℝ) * w (L+1) (a+1) b i
        + ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (L+1+1), w (L+1) (a+1) b i := by
    rw [← Finset.sum_add_distrib]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intros
    push_cast
    ring
  have h4 := shift L (a+1) b (fun _ => 1)
  simp only [one_mul] at h4
  have h5 := w_sum (a+1+1) b L
  have h6 := w_sum (a+1) b (L+1)
  have h3 := rise_shift (a+1+b) L
  have e : a+1+b+1 = a+b+2 := by ring
  have e2 : a+1+b = a+b+1 := by ring
  have e3 : a+1+1+b = a+b+2 := by ring
  rw [e, e2] at h3
  rw [e2] at h6
  rw [e3] at h5
  rw [h5] at h4
  rw [h6, h3] at hs
  have hs' : ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (L+1+1), ((i:ℝ)) * w (L+1) (a+1) b i
      = ((L:ℝ)+1) * (a+1) * rise (a+b+2) L := by
    have : ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (L+2), (i:ℝ) * w (L+1) (a+1) b i
      = ((L:ℝ)+1) * (a+1) * rise (a+b+2) L := h4
    exact this
  show ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+1+2), (k:ℝ) * ((k:ℝ) * w (L+1+1) a b k) = _
  rw [h]
  beta_reduce at h
  rw [hs, hs']
  push_cast
  ring

lemma prior_eq (N : ℕ) (a b : ℝ) (k : ℕ) : prior N a b k = w N a b k / rise (a+b) N := rfl

lemma E0 {a b : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 < b) (L : ℕ) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), prior (L+2) a b k = 1 := by
  have hZ : rise (a+b) (L+2) ≠ 0 := (rise_pos (by linarith) _).ne'
  have := w_sum a b (L+2)
  simp only [prior_eq]
  rw [← Finset.sum_div]
  exact (div_eq_one_iff_eq hZ).2 this

lemma E1 {a b : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 < b) (L : ℕ) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), prior (L+2) a b k * (k:ℝ) = ((L:ℝ)+2) * a / (a+b) := by
  have hr : rise (a+b+2) L ≠ 0 := (rise_pos (by linarith) _).ne'
  have e : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), prior (L+2) a b k * (k:ℝ)
      = (∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), (k:ℝ) * w (L+2) a b k) / rise (a+b) (L+2) := by
    rw [Finset.sum_div]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intros
    rw [prior_eq]
    ring
  rw [e, S1, Zr]
  have : a + b ≠ 0 := by linarith
  have : a + b + 1 ≠ 0 := by linarith
  field_simp

lemma E2 {a b : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 < b) (L : ℕ) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), prior (L+2) a b k * (k:ℝ)^2
      = ((L:ℝ)+2) * a * (((L:ℝ)+1) * (a+1) + (a+b+1)) / ((a+b) * (a+b+1)) := by
  have hr : rise (a+b+2) L ≠ 0 := (rise_pos (by linarith) _).ne'
  have e : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), prior (L+2) a b k * (k:ℝ)^2
      = (∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), (k:ℝ) * ((k:ℝ) * w (L+2) a b k)) / rise (a+b) (L+2) := by
    rw [Finset.sum_div]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intros
    rw [prior_eq]
    ring
  rw [e, S2, Zr]
  have : a + b ≠ 0 := by linarith
  have : a + b + 1 ≠ 0 := by linarith
  field_simp

/-- General beta-binomial variance, proved from the definition of `prior`:
Var(K) = N a b (a+b+N) / ((a+b)^2 (a+b+1)), N = L+2 (mean is N a/(a+b)). -/
lemma var_formula {a b : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 < b) (L : ℕ) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), prior (L+2) a b k * ((k:ℝ) - ((L:ℝ)+2) * a / (a+b))^2
      = ((L:ℝ)+2) * a * b * (a+b+((L:ℝ)+2)) / ((a+b)^2 * (a+b+1)) := by
  set μ : ℝ := ((L:ℝ)+2) * a / (a+b) with hμ
  have e : ∀ k : ℕ, prior (L+2) a b k * ((k:ℝ) - μ)^2
      = prior (L+2) a b k * (k:ℝ)^2 - 2 * μ * (prior (L+2) a b k * (k:ℝ))
        + μ^2 * prior (L+2) a b k := by
    intro k; ring
  simp only [e, Finset.sum_add_distrib, Finset.sum_sub_distrib, ← Finset.mul_sum]
  rw [E2 ha hb, E1 ha hb, E0 ha hb, hμ]
  have : a + b ≠ 0 := by linarith
  have : a + b + 1 ≠ 0 := by linarith
  field_simp
  ring

/-- Parameters are determined by the supplied mean and sd (intermediate result). -/
lemma params {a b : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 < b)
    (hmean : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) / 50) = 3/4)
    (hvar : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) / 50 - 3/4)^2 = 1/100) :
    a = 213/10 ∧ b = 71/10 := by
  have h1 := E1 ha hb 48
  have hv := var_formula ha hb 48
  have hs : a + b ≠ 0 := by linarith
  have hm : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) / 50)
      = (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * (k:ℝ)) / 50 := by
    rw [Finset.sum_div]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intros
    ring
  have h1' : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * (k:ℝ) = 50 * a / (a + b) := by
    have := h1
    norm_num at this
    exact this
  rw [hm, h1'] at hmean
  have hab : a = 3 * b := by
    field_simp at hmean
    linarith
  have hμ : (50 : ℝ) * a / (a + b) = 75 / 2 := by
    rw [hab]; field_simp; ring
  have hv' : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) - 50 * a / (a + b))^2
      = 50 * a * b * (a + b + 50) / ((a+b)^2 * (a+b+1)) := by
    have := hv
    norm_num at this
    exact this
  rw [hμ] at hv'
  have hvar' : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) / 50 - 3/4)^2
      = (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) - 75/2)^2) / 2500 := by
    rw [Finset.sum_div]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intros
    ring
  rw [hvar', hv'] at hvar
  subst hab
  have hb2 : b ≠ 0 := hb.ne'
  have h4 : 3 * b + b + 1 ≠ 0 := by linarith
  field_simp at hvar
  have hb' : b = 71/10 := by
    have : b^2 * (b * 100000 - 7100 * 100) = 0 := by nlinarith [hvar]
    have hb2' : b^2 ≠ 0 := pow_ne_zero 2 hb2
    have := (mul_eq_zero.mp this).resolve_left hb2'
    linarith
  subst hb'
  norm_num

/-! ### Posterior predictive (general Polya-urn identity, proved from the definitions) -/

lemma Fdesc (b : ℝ) : ∀ n N : ℕ, ∀ a : ℝ, n ≤ N →
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N+1), ((k.descFactorial n : ℕ) : ℝ) * w N a b k
      = ((N.descFactorial n : ℕ) : ℝ) * rise a n * rise (a + n + b) (N - n) := by
  intro n
  induction n with
  | zero =>
    intro N a _
    simp only [Nat.descFactorial_zero, Nat.cast_one, one_mul, Nat.sub_zero, Nat.cast_zero,
      add_zero, rise_zero]
    exact w_sum a b N
  | succ n ih =>
    intro N a hn
    obtain ⟨M, rfl⟩ : ∃ M, N = M + 1 := ⟨N - 1, by omega⟩
    have h := shift M a b (fun k => ((( k - 1).descFactorial n : ℕ) : ℝ))
    have e : ∀ k : ℕ, ((k.descFactorial (n+1) : ℕ) : ℝ) * w (M+1) a b k
        = ((((k - 1).descFactorial n : ℕ)) : ℝ) * ((k:ℝ) * w (M+1) a b k) := by
      intro k
      cases k with
      | zero => simp
      | succ i =>
        rw [Nat.succ_descFactorial_succ]
        simp only [Nat.add_sub_cancel]
        push_cast
        ring
    have hL : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (M+1+1), ((k.descFactorial (n+1) : ℕ) : ℝ) * w (M+1) a b k
        = ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (M+2), ((((k - 1).descFactorial n : ℕ)) : ℝ) * ((k:ℝ) * w (M+1) a b k) :=
      Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun k _ => e k)
    rw [hL, h]
    simp only [Nat.add_sub_cancel]
    rw [ih M (a+1) (by omega), Nat.succ_descFactorial_succ, rise_shift a n]
    have e2 : M + 1 - (n + 1) = M - n := by omega
    rw [e2]
    have e3 : a + 1 + (n:ℝ) + b = a + ((n+1 : ℕ) : ℝ) + b := by push_cast; ring
    rw [e3]
    push_cast
    ring

/-- General result: after n black draws, the predictive probability is (a+n)/(a+b+n);
here specialised to N = 50, n = 10. -/
lemma predictive_formula {a b : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 < b) :
    predictive a b = (a + 10) / (a + b + 10) := by
  have hZ : rise (a+b) 50 ≠ 0 := (rise_pos (by linarith) _).ne'
  have hD : (((50:ℕ).descFactorial 10 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by
    norm_num [Nat.descFactorial]
  have hR : rise (a + 11 + b) 39 ≠ 0 := (rise_pos (by linarith) _).ne'
  have hA : rise a 10 ≠ 0 := (rise_pos ha _).ne'
  have hx : a + 10 + b ≠ 0 := by linarith
  have h11 : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, ((k.descFactorial 11 : ℕ) : ℝ) * w 50 a b k
      = (((50:ℕ).descFactorial 11 : ℕ) : ℝ) * rise a 11 * rise (a + 11 + b) 39 := by
    have h := Fdesc b 11 50 a (by norm_num)
    rw [show a + ((11:ℕ):ℝ) + b = a + 11 + b by push_cast; ring] at h
    exact h
  have h10 : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, ((k.descFactorial 10 : ℕ) : ℝ) * w 50 a b k
      = (((50:ℕ).descFactorial 10 : ℕ) : ℝ) * rise a 10 * rise (a + 10 + b) 40 := by
    have h := Fdesc b 10 50 a (by norm_num)
    rw [show a + ((10:ℕ):ℝ) + b = a + 10 + b by push_cast; ring] at h
    exact h
  have hd : (((50:ℕ).descFactorial 11 : ℕ) : ℝ) = 40 * (((50:ℕ).descFactorial 10 : ℕ) : ℝ) := by
    norm_num [Nat.descFactorial]
  have hr11 : rise a 11 = rise a 10 * (a + 10) := by
    have := rise_succ a 10
    norm_num at this
    exact this
  have hr40 : rise (a + 10 + b) 40 = (a + 10 + b) * rise (a + 11 + b) 39 := by
    have := rise_shift (a + 10 + b) 39
    have e : a + 10 + b + 1 = a + 11 + b := by ring
    rw [e] at this
    exact this
  have hk : ∀ k : ℕ, ((k.descFactorial 11 : ℕ) : ℝ) = ((k.descFactorial 10 : ℕ) : ℝ) * ((k:ℝ) - 10) := by
    intro k
    rcases Nat.lt_or_ge k 10 with h | h
    · have h0 : k.descFactorial 10 = 0 := Nat.descFactorial_eq_zero_iff_lt.mpr h
      have h1 : k.descFactorial 11 = 0 := Nat.descFactorial_eq_zero_iff_lt.mpr (by omega)
      rw [h0, h1]; simp
    · rw [show (11:ℕ) = 10 + 1 from rfl, Nat.descFactorial_succ, Nat.cast_mul, Nat.cast_sub h]
      push_cast
      ring
  have hnum : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * lik k * nextBlack k
      = (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, ((k.descFactorial 11 : ℕ) : ℝ) * w 50 a b k) / (rise (a+b) 50 * (((50:ℕ).descFactorial 10 : ℕ) : ℝ) * 40) := by
    rw [Finset.sum_div]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intro k _
    rw [prior_eq, lik, nextBlack, hk k]
    field_simp
  have hden : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * lik k
      = (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, ((k.descFactorial 10 : ℕ) : ℝ) * w 50 a b k) / (rise (a+b) 50 * (((50:ℕ).descFactorial 10 : ℕ) : ℝ)) := by
    rw [Finset.sum_div]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intro k _
    rw [prior_eq, lik]
    field_simp
  unfold predictive
  rw [hnum, hden]
  rw [h11, h10, hd, hr11, hr40]
  field_simp
  ring

/-! ### Final answer -/

/-- ANSWER.  If K has the beta-binomial prior (unspecified a,b>0) whose K/50 has mean 3/4 and
variance 1/100, then after 10 black draws without replacement the probability that the
next ball is black is 313/384 ≈ 0.815. -/
theorem answer_theorem : ∀ a b : ℝ, 0 < a → 0 < b →
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) / 50) = 3/4 →
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) / 50 - 3/4)^2 = 1/100 →
    predictive a b = 313/384 := by
  intro a b ha hb hm hv
  obtain ⟨rfl, rfl⟩ := params ha hb hm hv
  rw [predictive_formula ha hb]
  norm_num
checks/6ecce009375a4db9ace0f8950a9032ce/submitted.lean
import Mathlib.Data.Real.Basic
import Mathlib.Data.Nat.Choose.Basic
import Mathlib.Data.Nat.Factorial.Basic
import Mathlib.Algebra.BigOperators.Intervals
import Mathlib.Algebra.BigOperators.Ring.Finset
import Mathlib.Algebra.BigOperators.Field
import Mathlib.Tactic.NormNum
import Mathlib.Tactic.FieldSimp
import Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith
import Mathlib.Tactic.Ring
import Mathlib.Tactic.Positivity
import Mathlib.Tactic.LinearCombination

/-!
# Beta-binomial model for the black count of a 50-ball box

SUPPLIED: box of N = 50 balls; K = unknown number of black ones; 10 balls drawn without
replacement, all black; across boxes the fraction K/50 has mean 3/4 and sd 1/10.

MODEL CHOICE (assumption, for review): across boxes K follows a beta-binomial law with
UNSPECIFIED positive parameters a, b:
   P(K = k) = C(N,k) * a^(k rising) * b^((N-k) rising) / (a+b)^(N rising)
(= the law of K | p ~ Binomial(N,p) with p ~ Beta(a,b)).  a, b are NOT assumed: they are
derived below from the supplied mean and sd.  Given K = k, the 10 draws are a uniformly
random sample without replacement, and my box is exchangeable with the other boxes.
-/

/-- rising factorial x (x+1) ... (x+n-1) -/
def rise (x : ℝ) : ℕ → ℝ
  | 0 => 1
  | n+1 => rise x n * (x + n)

/-- Beta-binomial prior pmf of the initial black count k, box size N, parameters a b. -/
noncomputable def prior (N : ℕ) (a b : ℝ) (k : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  (N.choose k : ℝ) * rise a k * rise b (N - k) / rise (a + b) N

/-- Likelihood of 10 black draws without replacement if k of 50 balls are black. -/
noncomputable def lik (k : ℕ) : ℝ := (k.descFactorial 10 : ℝ) / ((50 : ℕ).descFactorial 10 : ℝ)

/-- Chance the 11th ball is black given k black initially and 10 black removed (40 left). -/
noncomputable def nextBlack (k : ℕ) : ℝ := ((k : ℝ) - 10) / 40

/-- Posterior predictive probability that the next ball is black. -/
noncomputable def predictive (a b : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * lik k * nextBlack k) /
  (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * lik k)

/-! ### Technical lemmas (proved, not assumed) -/

lemma rise_succ (x : ℝ) (n : ℕ) : rise x (n+1) = rise x n * (x + n) := rfl

lemma rise_zero (x : ℝ) : rise x 0 = 1 := rfl

lemma rise_pos {x : ℝ} (hx : 0 < x) : ∀ n, 0 < rise x n := by
  intro n
  induction n with
  | zero => simp [rise]
  | succ n ih =>
    rw [rise_succ]
    have : (0:ℝ) ≤ n := Nat.cast_nonneg n
    exact mul_pos ih (by linarith)

lemma rise_shift (x : ℝ) : ∀ n, rise x (n+1) = x * rise (x+1) n := by
  intro n
  induction n with
  | zero => simp [rise]
  | succ n ih =>
    rw [rise_succ x (n+1), ih, rise_succ (x+1) n]
    push_cast
    ring

/-- unnormalised weight -/
noncomputable def w (N : ℕ) (a b : ℝ) (k : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  (N.choose k : ℝ) * rise a k * rise b (N - k)

lemma w_succ_sum (a b : ℝ) (N : ℕ) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N+2), w (N+1) a b k
      = (a + b + N) * ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N+1), w N a b k := by
  have h1 : ∀ i : ℕ, w (N+1) a b (i+1)
      = w N a b i * (a + i) + w N a b (i+1) * (b + N - (i+1)) := by
    intro i
    unfold w
    rw [Nat.choose_succ_succ, Nat.add_sub_add_right, rise_succ a i]
    rcases Nat.lt_or_ge i N with h | h
    · have e : N - i = (N - (i+1)) + 1 := by omega
      rw [e, rise_succ b (N-(i+1)), Nat.cast_sub (by omega : i+1 ≤ N)]
      push_cast
      ring
    · have h0 : N.choose (i+1) = 0 := Nat.choose_eq_zero_of_lt (by omega)
      rw [h0]
      push_cast
      ring
  have hz : w N a b (N+1) = 0 := by simp [w]
  have hA : ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (N+1), w N a b (i+1) * (b + N - (i+1))
      = ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N+1), w N a b k * (b + N - k) - rise b (N+1) := by
    have := Finset.sum_range_succ' (fun k => w N a b k * (b + N - k)) (N+1)
    rw [Finset.sum_range_succ] at this
    have h0 : w N a b 0 * (b + N - ((0:ℕ):ℝ)) = rise b (N+1) := by
      simp [w, rise_succ, rise_zero]
    simp only [hz, zero_mul, add_zero, h0] at this
    push_cast at this ⊢
    linarith
  rw [Finset.sum_range_succ' _ (N+1)]
  simp only [h1, Finset.sum_add_distrib]
  rw [hA]
  have h00 : w (N+1) a b 0 = rise b (N+1) := by simp [w, rise_zero]
  rw [h00]
  have key : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N+1), w N a b k * (a + k)
      + ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N+1), w N a b k * (b + N - k)
      = (a + b + N) * ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N+1), w N a b k := by
    rw [Finset.mul_sum, ← Finset.sum_add_distrib]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intros
    ring
  linarith

/-- The prior is normalised: Vandermonde identity for rising factorials. -/
lemma w_sum (a b : ℝ) : ∀ N : ℕ, ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N+1), w N a b k = rise (a+b) N := by
  intro N
  induction N with
  | zero => simp [w, rise]
  | succ n ih =>
    rw [w_succ_sum, ih, rise_succ]
    ring

lemma shift (M : ℕ) (a b : ℝ) (f : ℕ → ℝ) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (M+2), f k * ((k:ℝ) * w (M+1) a b k)
      = ((M:ℝ)+1) * a * ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (M+1), f (i+1) * w M (a+1) b i := by
  rw [Finset.sum_range_succ']
  simp only [Nat.cast_zero, zero_mul, mul_zero, add_zero]
  rw [Finset.mul_sum]
  apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
  intro i _
  have hc := Nat.add_one_mul_choose_eq M i
  have hc' : ((M:ℝ)+1) * (M.choose i : ℝ) = ((M+1).choose (i+1) : ℝ) * ((i:ℝ)+1) := by
    exact_mod_cast hc
  unfold w
  rw [Nat.add_sub_add_right, rise_shift a i]
  push_cast
  linear_combination (-(f (i+1)) * a * rise (a+1) i * rise b (M-i)) * hc'

lemma Zr (a b : ℝ) (L : ℕ) :
    rise (a+b) (L+2) = (a+b) * (a+b+1) * rise (a+b+2) L := by
  rw [rise_shift (a+b) (L+1), rise_shift (a+b+1) L]
  have : a+b+1+1 = a+b+2 := by ring
  rw [this]
  ring

lemma S1 (a b : ℝ) (L : ℕ) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), (k:ℝ) * w (L+2) a b k
      = ((L:ℝ)+2) * a * ((a+b+1) * rise (a+b+2) L) := by
  have h := shift (L+1) a b (fun _ => 1)
  simp only [one_mul] at h
  have h2 := w_sum (a+1) b (L+1)
  have h3 := rise_shift (a+1+b) L
  have e : a+1+b+1 = a+b+2 := by ring
  have e2 : a+1+b = a+b+1 := by ring
  rw [e, e2] at h3
  rw [e2] at h2
  rw [h2, h3] at h
  push_cast at h
  convert h using 2
  ring

lemma S2 (a b : ℝ) (L : ℕ) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), (k:ℝ) * ((k:ℝ) * w (L+2) a b k)
      = ((L:ℝ)+2) * a * (((L:ℝ)+1) * (a+1) * rise (a+b+2) L
          + (a+b+1) * rise (a+b+2) L) := by
  have h := shift (L+1) a b (fun k => (k:ℝ))
  have hs : ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (L+1+1), ((i+1:ℕ):ℝ) * w (L+1) (a+1) b i
      = ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (L+1+1), (i:ℝ) * w (L+1) (a+1) b i
        + ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (L+1+1), w (L+1) (a+1) b i := by
    rw [← Finset.sum_add_distrib]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intros
    push_cast
    ring
  have h4 := shift L (a+1) b (fun _ => 1)
  simp only [one_mul] at h4
  have h5 := w_sum (a+1+1) b L
  have h6 := w_sum (a+1) b (L+1)
  have h3 := rise_shift (a+1+b) L
  have e : a+1+b+1 = a+b+2 := by ring
  have e2 : a+1+b = a+b+1 := by ring
  have e3 : a+1+1+b = a+b+2 := by ring
  rw [e, e2] at h3
  rw [e2] at h6
  rw [e3] at h5
  rw [h5] at h4
  rw [h6, h3] at hs
  have hs' : ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (L+1+1), ((i:ℝ)) * w (L+1) (a+1) b i
      = ((L:ℝ)+1) * (a+1) * rise (a+b+2) L := by
    have : ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (L+2), (i:ℝ) * w (L+1) (a+1) b i
      = ((L:ℝ)+1) * (a+1) * rise (a+b+2) L := h4
    exact this
  show ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+1+2), (k:ℝ) * ((k:ℝ) * w (L+1+1) a b k) = _
  rw [h]
  beta_reduce at h
  rw [hs, hs']
  push_cast
  ring

lemma prior_eq (N : ℕ) (a b : ℝ) (k : ℕ) : prior N a b k = w N a b k / rise (a+b) N := rfl

lemma E0 {a b : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 < b) (L : ℕ) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), prior (L+2) a b k = 1 := by
  have hZ : rise (a+b) (L+2) ≠ 0 := (rise_pos (by linarith) _).ne'
  have := w_sum a b (L+2)
  simp only [prior_eq]
  rw [← Finset.sum_div]
  exact (div_eq_one_iff_eq hZ).2 this

lemma E1 {a b : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 < b) (L : ℕ) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), prior (L+2) a b k * (k:ℝ) = ((L:ℝ)+2) * a / (a+b) := by
  have hr : rise (a+b+2) L ≠ 0 := (rise_pos (by linarith) _).ne'
  have e : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), prior (L+2) a b k * (k:ℝ)
      = (∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), (k:ℝ) * w (L+2) a b k) / rise (a+b) (L+2) := by
    rw [Finset.sum_div]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intros
    rw [prior_eq]
    ring
  rw [e, S1, Zr]
  have : a + b ≠ 0 := by linarith
  have : a + b + 1 ≠ 0 := by linarith
  field_simp

lemma E2 {a b : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 < b) (L : ℕ) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), prior (L+2) a b k * (k:ℝ)^2
      = ((L:ℝ)+2) * a * (((L:ℝ)+1) * (a+1) + (a+b+1)) / ((a+b) * (a+b+1)) := by
  have hr : rise (a+b+2) L ≠ 0 := (rise_pos (by linarith) _).ne'
  have e : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), prior (L+2) a b k * (k:ℝ)^2
      = (∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), (k:ℝ) * ((k:ℝ) * w (L+2) a b k)) / rise (a+b) (L+2) := by
    rw [Finset.sum_div]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intros
    rw [prior_eq]
    ring
  rw [e, S2, Zr]
  have : a + b ≠ 0 := by linarith
  have : a + b + 1 ≠ 0 := by linarith
  field_simp

/-- General beta-binomial variance, proved from the definition of `prior`:
Var(K) = N a b (a+b+N) / ((a+b)^2 (a+b+1)), N = L+2 (mean is N a/(a+b)). -/
lemma var_formula {a b : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 < b) (L : ℕ) :
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (L+3), prior (L+2) a b k * ((k:ℝ) - ((L:ℝ)+2) * a / (a+b))^2
      = ((L:ℝ)+2) * a * b * (a+b+((L:ℝ)+2)) / ((a+b)^2 * (a+b+1)) := by
  set μ : ℝ := ((L:ℝ)+2) * a / (a+b) with hμ
  have e : ∀ k : ℕ, prior (L+2) a b k * ((k:ℝ) - μ)^2
      = prior (L+2) a b k * (k:ℝ)^2 - 2 * μ * (prior (L+2) a b k * (k:ℝ))
        + μ^2 * prior (L+2) a b k := by
    intro k; ring
  simp only [e, Finset.sum_add_distrib, Finset.sum_sub_distrib, ← Finset.mul_sum]
  rw [E2 ha hb, E1 ha hb, E0 ha hb, hμ]
  have : a + b ≠ 0 := by linarith
  have : a + b + 1 ≠ 0 := by linarith
  field_simp
  ring

/-- Parameters are determined by the supplied mean and sd (intermediate result). -/
lemma params {a b : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 < b)
    (hmean : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) / 50) = 3/4)
    (hvar : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) / 50 - 3/4)^2 = 1/100) :
    a = 213/10 ∧ b = 71/10 := by
  have h1 := E1 ha hb 48
  have hv := var_formula ha hb 48
  have hs : a + b ≠ 0 := by linarith
  have hm : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) / 50)
      = (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * (k:ℝ)) / 50 := by
    rw [Finset.sum_div]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intros
    ring
  have h1' : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * (k:ℝ) = 50 * a / (a + b) := by
    have := h1
    norm_num at this
    exact this
  rw [hm, h1'] at hmean
  have hab : a = 3 * b := by
    field_simp at hmean
    linarith
  have hμ : (50 : ℝ) * a / (a + b) = 75 / 2 := by
    rw [hab]; field_simp; ring
  have hv' : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) - 50 * a / (a + b))^2
      = 50 * a * b * (a + b + 50) / ((a+b)^2 * (a+b+1)) := by
    have := hv
    norm_num at this
    exact this
  rw [hμ] at hv'
  have hvar' : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) / 50 - 3/4)^2
      = (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) - 75/2)^2) / 2500 := by
    rw [Finset.sum_div]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intros
    ring
  rw [hvar', hv'] at hvar
  subst hab
  have hb2 : b ≠ 0 := hb.ne'
  have h4 : 3 * b + b + 1 ≠ 0 := by linarith
  field_simp at hvar
  have hb' : b = 71/10 := by
    have : b^2 * (b * 100000 - 7100 * 100) = 0 := by nlinarith [hvar]
    have hb2' : b^2 ≠ 0 := pow_ne_zero 2 hb2
    have := (mul_eq_zero.mp this).resolve_left hb2'
    linarith
  subst hb'
  norm_num

/-! ### Posterior predictive (general Polya-urn identity, proved from the definitions) -/

lemma Fdesc (b : ℝ) : ∀ n N : ℕ, ∀ a : ℝ, n ≤ N →
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (N+1), ((k.descFactorial n : ℕ) : ℝ) * w N a b k
      = ((N.descFactorial n : ℕ) : ℝ) * rise a n * rise (a + n + b) (N - n) := by
  intro n
  induction n with
  | zero =>
    intro N a _
    simp only [Nat.descFactorial_zero, Nat.cast_one, one_mul, Nat.sub_zero, Nat.cast_zero,
      add_zero, rise_zero]
    exact w_sum a b N
  | succ n ih =>
    intro N a hn
    obtain ⟨M, rfl⟩ : ∃ M, N = M + 1 := ⟨N - 1, by omega⟩
    have h := shift M a b (fun k => ((( k - 1).descFactorial n : ℕ) : ℝ))
    have e : ∀ k : ℕ, ((k.descFactorial (n+1) : ℕ) : ℝ) * w (M+1) a b k
        = ((((k - 1).descFactorial n : ℕ)) : ℝ) * ((k:ℝ) * w (M+1) a b k) := by
      intro k
      cases k with
      | zero => simp
      | succ i =>
        rw [Nat.succ_descFactorial_succ]
        simp only [Nat.add_sub_cancel]
        push_cast
        ring
    have hL : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (M+1+1), ((k.descFactorial (n+1) : ℕ) : ℝ) * w (M+1) a b k
        = ∑ k ∈ Finset.range (M+2), ((((k - 1).descFactorial n : ℕ)) : ℝ) * ((k:ℝ) * w (M+1) a b k) :=
      Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun k _ => e k)
    rw [hL, h]
    simp only [Nat.add_sub_cancel]
    rw [ih M (a+1) (by omega), Nat.succ_descFactorial_succ, rise_shift a n]
    have e2 : M + 1 - (n + 1) = M - n := by omega
    rw [e2]
    have e3 : a + 1 + (n:ℝ) + b = a + ((n+1 : ℕ) : ℝ) + b := by push_cast; ring
    rw [e3]
    push_cast
    ring

/-- General result: after n black draws, the predictive probability is (a+n)/(a+b+n);
here specialised to N = 50, n = 10. -/
lemma predictive_formula {a b : ℝ} (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 < b) :
    predictive a b = (a + 10) / (a + b + 10) := by
  have hZ : rise (a+b) 50 ≠ 0 := (rise_pos (by linarith) _).ne'
  have hD : (((50:ℕ).descFactorial 10 : ℕ) : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by
    norm_num [Nat.descFactorial]
  have hR : rise (a + 11 + b) 39 ≠ 0 := (rise_pos (by linarith) _).ne'
  have hA : rise a 10 ≠ 0 := (rise_pos ha _).ne'
  have hx : a + 10 + b ≠ 0 := by linarith
  have h11 : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, ((k.descFactorial 11 : ℕ) : ℝ) * w 50 a b k
      = (((50:ℕ).descFactorial 11 : ℕ) : ℝ) * rise a 11 * rise (a + 11 + b) 39 := by
    have h := Fdesc b 11 50 a (by norm_num)
    rw [show a + ((11:ℕ):ℝ) + b = a + 11 + b by push_cast; ring] at h
    exact h
  have h10 : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, ((k.descFactorial 10 : ℕ) : ℝ) * w 50 a b k
      = (((50:ℕ).descFactorial 10 : ℕ) : ℝ) * rise a 10 * rise (a + 10 + b) 40 := by
    have h := Fdesc b 10 50 a (by norm_num)
    rw [show a + ((10:ℕ):ℝ) + b = a + 10 + b by push_cast; ring] at h
    exact h
  have hd : (((50:ℕ).descFactorial 11 : ℕ) : ℝ) = 40 * (((50:ℕ).descFactorial 10 : ℕ) : ℝ) := by
    norm_num [Nat.descFactorial]
  have hr11 : rise a 11 = rise a 10 * (a + 10) := by
    have := rise_succ a 10
    norm_num at this
    exact this
  have hr40 : rise (a + 10 + b) 40 = (a + 10 + b) * rise (a + 11 + b) 39 := by
    have := rise_shift (a + 10 + b) 39
    have e : a + 10 + b + 1 = a + 11 + b := by ring
    rw [e] at this
    exact this
  have hk : ∀ k : ℕ, ((k.descFactorial 11 : ℕ) : ℝ) = ((k.descFactorial 10 : ℕ) : ℝ) * ((k:ℝ) - 10) := by
    intro k
    rcases Nat.lt_or_ge k 10 with h | h
    · have h0 : k.descFactorial 10 = 0 := Nat.descFactorial_eq_zero_iff_lt.mpr h
      have h1 : k.descFactorial 11 = 0 := Nat.descFactorial_eq_zero_iff_lt.mpr (by omega)
      rw [h0, h1]; simp
    · rw [show (11:ℕ) = 10 + 1 from rfl, Nat.descFactorial_succ, Nat.cast_mul, Nat.cast_sub h]
      push_cast
      ring
  have hnum : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * lik k * nextBlack k
      = (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, ((k.descFactorial 11 : ℕ) : ℝ) * w 50 a b k) / (rise (a+b) 50 * (((50:ℕ).descFactorial 10 : ℕ) : ℝ) * 40) := by
    rw [Finset.sum_div]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intro k _
    rw [prior_eq, lik, nextBlack, hk k]
    field_simp
  have hden : ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * lik k
      = (∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, ((k.descFactorial 10 : ℕ) : ℝ) * w 50 a b k) / (rise (a+b) 50 * (((50:ℕ).descFactorial 10 : ℕ) : ℝ)) := by
    rw [Finset.sum_div]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intro k _
    rw [prior_eq, lik]
    field_simp
  unfold predictive
  rw [hnum, hden]
  rw [h11, h10, hd, hr11, hr40]
  field_simp
  ring

/-! ### Final answer -/

/-- ANSWER.  If K has the beta-binomial prior (unspecified a,b>0) whose K/50 has mean 3/4 and
variance 1/100, then after 10 black draws without replacement the probability that the
next ball is black is 313/384 ≈ 0.815. -/
theorem answer_theorem : ∀ a b : ℝ, 0 < a → 0 < b →
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) / 50) = 3/4 →
    ∑ k ∈ Finset.range 51, prior 50 a b k * ((k:ℝ) / 50 - 3/4)^2 = 1/100 →
    predictive a b = 313/384 := by
  intro a b ha hb hm hv
  obtain ⟨rfl, rfl⟩ := params ha hb hm hv
  rw [predictive_formula ha hb]
  norm_num
